# The burnout accuracy envelope (W9, §7 item 12(2))

Produced by `test/newcore/gates/burnout_envelope.jl` at commit 4a35494 (tree aa0f30f). Julia 1.13.0, 1 thread per process, 4 process(es); one-minute load average 10.0, 10.4, 11.0, 11.6 at the start and 11.3, 11.3, 9.7, 14.1 at the end; the longest process 4203 s. 630 runs, 130 families. The finite-volume core with the equidistribution correction at its default layer time (`ActivationTime()`, λ = 1.8); the script's header defines every deck, run and measure.

## The envelope

The hard criterion per family (a deck, threshold, band, tolerance and activation at its four resolutions, the default receiver): pass over every resolution, "from N" over the finest three, or fail with the failing QoIs named; the invocations at each resolution in brackets. Every QoI within tolerance; (a) the largest per-step excess of N over 2N and (b) the successive L1, each falling at least 1.5× per doubling; the accepted-step peak converging.
